- key_excerpts: [{"type":"funding","text":"Meta 与贝莱德成立合资企业，投资约 140 亿美元在美国得克萨斯州埃尔帕索建设一座 1 吉瓦计算能力的数据中心园区。贝莱德管理的基金持有合资公司 80% 股权，Meta 持有 20%，项目计划于 2028 年开始逐步投入运营，Meta 将成为首个且唯一的入驻用户。该园区是 Meta 超过 100 亿美元的投资，建设高峰期预计创造超 4000 个岗位。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"funding","text":"Meta 联合贝莱德成立合资企业，投资 140 亿美元建设得州 AI 数据中心 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 Meta 联合贝莱德成立合资企业，投资 140 亿美元建设得州 AI 数据中心 2026/7/28 17:54:32 来源： IT之家 作者： 远洋 责编： 远洋 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 华南吴彦祖 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，Meta Platforms 与贝莱德（BlackRock）今日宣布成立一家合资企业，共同开发并持有位于美国得克萨斯州埃尔帕索（El Paso）的一座数据中心园区。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Meta 已拥有超过 15 年的数据中心开发、建设和运营经验。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"其 Meta Compute 战略正是在这一基础上发展而来，将自身的基础设施建设能力与资本合作伙伴相结合，以满足其长期 AI 战略对建设速度和灵活性的需求。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"作为全球领先的资产管理公司，贝莱德及其旗下的全球基础设施合伙公司（Global Infrastructure Partners，GIP）和 HPS Investment Partners，将为该战略提供支持。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"MCP 2026-07-28 规范正式发布，协议核心从双向有状态转变为请求/响应无状态协议，每个请求自描述且可落在轮询负载均衡器后的任意实例上。新特性包括基于 HTTP 头的路由、可缓存列表响应、Multi Round-Trip Requests （MRTR） 替代服务器发起的流请求，以及授权强化（RFC 9207 issuer 验证、向 CIMD 迁移）。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"Table of Contents What changed No handshake or sessions Multi Round-Trip Requests (MRTR) Header-based routing List results are cacheable Authorization Tasks Deprecations SDKs Ecosystem support Getting started Thank you Since our last November release MCP continued to grow at an astonishing rate.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"number","text":"Across our Tier 1 SDKs, we&rsquo;re seeing close to half-a-billion downloads a month, with both TypeScript and Python SDKs crossing the 1 billion total downloads threshold.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"In just a few months, the protocol continued to grow as the data and interactivity substrate for agentic workflows.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"Today, we&rsquo;re officially pushing the release button on the next version of the MCP specification, 2026-07-28 , along with the SDKs that will allow you to start building clients and servers right away.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"The highlight of this release is a stateless protocol core - MCP is transforming from a bidirectional stateful protocol into a request/response stateless protocol.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"微软 FY26 财年客户从 AI 实验转向实际业务部署，涌现出将 AI 嵌入核心运营的\"前沿企业\"。Atos 为 5.6 万名员工部署 Microsoft 365 Copilot，并构建了 1.9 万个 AI 智能体的生态系统。Banco Popular Dominicano 通过 AURA 智能体将运营风险监控覆盖率从 40% 提升至 100%，手动工作量减少 70%。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Throughout this past fiscal year, customers across every industry and segment moved from AI experimentation to deploying AI for real-world business outcomes.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"They unlocked innovation and created new opportunities for growth.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"We saw the emergence of Frontier Firms as they moved beyond efficiency gains to focus on human ambition and embed AI at the core of how they operate.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"Successful customers are building an intelligence platform so their unique IQ — their knowledge, data, workflows, applications and expertise — can continuously compound, ensuring the value of AI accrues to the customer, not the model.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"workflow_change","text":"They have a trust platform that is pervasive, with the ability to manage, govern, secure and measure AI across every business process.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"Fireworks AI 推出 Fireworks Nexus，一个即插即用的路由与成本控制层，可将开发者日常编码工作从昂贵模型转移至开源模型。该平台直接连接现有编码工具与托管开源模型层，旨在解决企业 AI 预算超支问题--据 Forbes 报道，Uber 在四个月内耗尽了其 2026 年全部 AI 预算。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"Editors Pick Agentic AI Artificial Intelligence AI Infrastructure Technology AI Shorts Applications Language Model New Releases Software Engineering Tech News Fireworks AI has released Fireworks Nexus , an AI management and routing platform aimed at engineering organizations.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"It connects the coding tools developers already use to a managed layer of open-weight models.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"The problem it targets is well documented.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Forbes reported that Uber exhausted its entire 2026 AI budget in four months.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"Claude Code had reached roughly 5,000 engineers after a December rollout.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"月之暗面发布Kimi K3，一个2.8T参数的开源权重模型，是目前最大的开源模型。该模型是去年Kimi Linear的规模化版本，新增LatentMoE组件，并全面采用NoPE（无位置嵌入）替代RoPE。其注意力残差连接在训练和推理成本上分别增加约4%和2%，但持续改善了验证损失和下游性能。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"The Kimi K3 architecture figure for yesterday’s big open-weight model release, along with some observations and thoughts.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"product_update","text":"Yes, it looks relatively complicated, but it’s essentially a scaled-up production version of their Kimi Linear model they released last year (scaled up from 48B -> 2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"8T; K3 is by far the biggest open-weight model right now) The one new component compared to Kimi Linear is the LatentMoE .","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"I omitted it in the figure below since it’s already very crowded, but that’s essentially the same LatentMoE as in Nemotron 3 Ultra (you can find it in my LLM Architecture Gallery if you are curious).","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"},{"type":"company_action","text":"The idea here is to compress (down-project) large linear layers similar to multi-head latent attention .","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"high"}]
- evidence_seed: {"company_actions":["The Kimi K3 architecture figure for yesterday’s big open-weight model release, along with some observations and thoughts.","Yes, it looks relatively complicated, but it’s essentially a scaled-up production version of their Kimi Linear model they released last year (scaled up from 48B -> 2.","8T; K3 is by far the biggest open-weight model right now) The one new component compared to Kimi Linear is the LatentMoE ."],"case_details":[],"workflow_changes":[],"before_after_clues":["可能涉及 计费 / 预算管理 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。"],"affected_roles":[],"risks_or_constraints":[]}

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"多家 AI 公司通过中间商大规模收购 2022 年前出版的二手图书，以获取未受 AI 生成内容污染的高质量训练数据。Anthropic 曾投入数百万美元购买纸质图书用于训练 Claude，随后将图书销毁，并因长期保存含 700 万本盗版图书的数据库被判赔偿 15 亿美元。订单规模从 1000 本到 100 万本不等，部分图书被采用破坏性扫描方式数字化后拆毁，引发伦理争议。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，AI 公司在推动内存和存储资源日益紧张之后，如今似乎又将目光投向了人类的文学遗产。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"调查媒体 404 Media 最新报道称，多家 AI 公司正通过中间商大规模收购二手图书，以获取高质量训练数据用于训练 AI 模型，同时避免引发公众舆论反弹。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"AI 的发展离不开海量数据，但并非所有数据都有价值，关键在于数据质量。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"近年来，大量由 AI 生成的低质量内容（俗称“AI 垃圾内容”）充斥互联网，不仅污染了数据环境，也降低了 AI 继续学习的效果。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"因此，领先的 AI 公司开始重新寻找由人类创作的内容，尤其是 2022 年之前出版的纸质书籍，因为这些内容更有可能是原创作品，尚未受到 AI 生成内容的污染。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"OpenAI 研究员 Jeffrey Wang 称赞 AMD 与 Cerebras 联合开发的 AI 推理解决方案表现\"令人难以置信\"。该方案整合 AMD Helios 机架与 Cerebras 晶圆级引擎，分别处理提示词和 Token 生成环节，预估可将每瓦每秒 token 数量提高 5 倍。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"OpenAI 研究员盛赞 AMD 联合 AI 推理方案：表现“令人难以置信” - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > IT资讯 > 业界 OpenAI 研究员盛赞 AMD 联合 AI 推理方案：表现“令人难以置信” 2026/7/29 9:43:48 来源： IT之家 作者： 故渊 责编： 故渊 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 华南吴彦祖 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 29 日消息，科技媒体 Wccftech 昨日（7 月 28 日）发布博文，报道称 OpenAI 公司研究员 Jeffrey Wang 指出， AMD 携手 Cerebras 公司联合开发 AI 推理解决方案表现“令人难以置信”。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家此前报道，该解决方案主要针对超低延迟 AI 推理场景，将整合 AMD 的 Helios 机架级解决方案与 Cerebras 的晶圆级引擎，从而应对英伟达和 Groq 合作。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"按照双方披露的路线，Helios 机架承担高性能、可扩展吞吐引擎角色，主要处理提示词与大上下文窗口。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"Cerebras 的 Wafer-Scale Engine 则负责对内存带宽要求更高的 Token 生成与解码环节，强调超低时延表现，结合使用 2 个计算引擎，预估可以将每瓦每秒 token 数量提高 5 倍。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"亚马逊正逐步淡化旗下多数Nova系列旗舰模型，将资源集中投入代号为FMR的前沿模型项目。该项目由研究员Pieter Abbeel负责，已成为AI业务最高优先级，新旗舰基础模型预计今年秋季AWS re：Invent大会发布。亚马逊同时关闭了2024年成立的AGI Lab，并对AGI部门进行了裁员和重组。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，据 Business Insider 报道，知情人士透露，亚马逊正在对其人工智能战略进行大规模调整，包括逐步停止开发多款自研 AI 模型、重组相关团队，并将更多工程师和资源集中投入到一项全新的前沿模型战略，以增强公司在顶级 AI 模型领域的竞争力。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家注意到，此次调整紧随亚马逊上周对通用人工智能（AGI）部门裁员，以及关闭 AGI Lab 研究团队之后。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"AGI Lab 成立于 2024 年，当时亚马逊吸纳了 AI 初创公司 Adept 的大部分核心团队，并以此组建了这一研究机构。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"这轮重组意味着亚马逊正在重新聚焦 AI 战略。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"与过去同时布局文本、图像和视频等多个模型方向不同， 公司如今正将工程人才和有限的算力资源集中投入到最优先的项目上。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"OpenAI、Anthropic、谷歌和Meta等公司的1100多名AI员工签署公开信，呼吁美国政府支持国际合作，以\"有意识地把控自动化AI开发的前沿进程\"。该倡议名为\"把控前沿\"，重点关注AI未来可能自行开发和改进AI系统的\"递归式自我改进\"能力。OpenAI CEO萨姆·奥尔特曼在播客采访中表示，可能需要\"把控\"AI发展速度，让社会有时间建立防护机制。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"北京时间 7 月 29 日，据《商业内幕》报道，美国一些头部 AI 公司的高管表示，联邦政府现在是时候找到一种方法，在必要时对 AI 开发踩刹车了。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"AI 业界呼吁控制 AI 发展 来自 OpenAI、Anthropic、谷歌和 Meta 等公司的 1100 多名 AI 员工联合签署了一封公开信，呼吁美国政府控制 AI 发展速度。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"眼下，AI 公司正竞相研发能力越来越强大的 AI 系统，让外界担忧 AI 的发展速度可能会超过现有安全保障、治理体系和监管机制所能跟上的步伐。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"“我们呼吁美国政府支持一项国际合作，开发必要的技术和治理工具，以便能够有意识地把控自动化 AI 开发的前沿进程。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"这个倡议名为“把控前沿”(Pacing the Frontier)，签署这封公开信的高管包括 Anthropic CEO 达里奥 · 阿莫代伊 (Dario Amodei) 以及公司两位联合创始人杰克 · 克拉克 (Jack Clark) 和贾里德 · 卡普兰 (Jared Kaplan)、OpenAI 首席科学家雅库布 · 帕霍茨基 (Jakub Pachocki)、Meta 首席科学家赵晟佳 (Shengjia Zhao)，以及负责谷歌 DeepMind AI 安全与对齐工作的安卡 · 德拉甘 (Anca Dragan)。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"case_detail","text":"比亚迪证实其人形机器人产品将于 8 月初在郑州迪空间首次面向公众亮相。比亚迪执行副总裁李柯此前表示，希望未来每家门店都能部署机器人，用于向顾客介绍和演示车辆，并认为面向家庭和服务行业的人形机器人将成为比亚迪的巨大市场。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"比亚迪人形机器人确认 8 月在郑州“上岗” - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 具身智能 比亚迪人形机器人确认 8 月在郑州“上岗” 2026/7/28 13:59:22 来源： IT之家 作者： 清源 责编： 清源 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 补药吖 、 HH_KK 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，7 月 25 日，比亚迪通过“比亚迪迪空间｜郑州馆”公众号发文暗示，机器人产品将于 8 月初首次面向公众亮相。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"随后，据财联社今天（28 日）下午报道，比亚迪方面证实， 人形机器人产品将于 8 月正式亮相 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"受此消息影响，比亚迪 A 股盘中快速拉升，一度涨超 2%；板块方面，人形机器人概念震荡拉升，明新旭腾 2 连板，上纬新材涨超 9%，北自科技、三花智控、拓斯达、力星股份、天奇股份跟涨。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"公开信息显示，“迪空间”也是比亚迪新技术、新产品的首发场景，郑州是首座迪空间的所在地。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"number","text":"月之暗面开源 2.8 万亿参数模型 Kimi K3，华为昇腾随即实现 0day 适配。训练侧基于 MindSpeed MM 在 Atlas 800 A3、Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD 完成适配并优化算子与显存；推理侧通过 vLLM Ascend 与 SGLang 部署，昇腾 950 超节点原生支持 Kimi K3 的 mxFP4 量化权重。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"华为官宣昇腾 0day 支持 Kimi K3 的训练适配及推理部署 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 华为官宣昇腾 0day 支持 Kimi K3 的训练适配及推理部署 2026/7/28 16:17:30 来源： IT之家 作者： 归泷 责编： 归泷 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 模块你就把 、 Autumn_Dream 、 不一样的体验 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息， 2026 年 7 月 27 日，月之暗面正式开源 Kimi K3 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"number","text":"8 万亿，是全球首个开源的 3 万亿级别模型，面向长程编程、知识工作和推理等前沿智能场景而设计。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"华为官方今日宣布： 此次 Kimi K3 一经开源发布，昇腾即实现 0day 极速适配 ：在训练侧，基于 MindSpeed MM 在 Atlas 800 A3、Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD 上完成减层训练基础功能适配，在算子、并行加速、显存优化等方面进行专项优化；在推理侧，通过 vLLM Ascend 与 SGLang 开源推理引擎实现快速部署，同时， 昇腾 950 超节点支持 FP8、MXFP8、MXFP4 等全系列数值精度格式 ，原生支持 Kimi K3 的 mxFP4 量化权重。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"据IT之家昨日报道，Kimi 官方还公布了支撑 Kimi K3 训练的三项 Infra 技术：MoonEP、FlashKDA 和 AgentEnv，覆盖从高性能通信、高性能算子到分布式 RL 环境的关键链路。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"阿里云 Qoder 上线实时语音交互智能体 QoderVoice，由全双工语音模型 Qwen-Audio-3.0-Realtime 驱动。用户可通过语音下达指令、讨论方案，智能体实时响应并执行任务，支持多轮协商与打断。QoderVoice 已登陆 Qoder 国际版，提供 3 天免费试用。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"阿里云 Qoder 上线实时语音交互智能体 QoderVoice，支持讨论式交互 - IT之家 首页 IT圈 最会买 设置 日夜间 随系统 浅色 深色 主题色 黑色 投稿 订阅 RSS订阅 收藏IT之家 软媒应用 App客户端 要知App 软媒魔方 业界 手机 电脑 测评 视频 AI 苹果 iPhone 鸿蒙 软件 智车 数码 学院 游戏 直播 5G 微软 Win10 Win11 专题 搜索 首页 > 智能时代 > 人工智能 阿里云 Qoder 上线实时语音交互智能体 QoderVoice，支持讨论式交互 2026/7/28 16:14:49 来源： IT之家 作者： 远洋 责编： 远洋 评论： 感谢IT之家网友 Domado 的线索投递！","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，今日，阿里云 Qoder 上线实时语音交互智能体 QoderVoice，由全双工语音模型 Qwen-Audio-3.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"用户可随时语音唤醒智能体，通过语音下达指令、讨论方案，智能体实时响应并执行任务。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家附官方详细介绍如下： 语音指令与任务执行同步进行 用户在 Qoder Quest 模式主界面点击语音入口，即可在任意界面唤起语音助手悬浮窗。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"发起语音指令后，Qoder 自动创建任务、调用工具并在后台执行。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"product_update","text":"火山引擎今日上线豆包搜索服务，为AI Agent提供跨语言、多模态联网信息查询，并融合字节跳动独家内容。该服务支持Agent直接使用的markdown与结构化数据输出，建立了信源权威分级体系以过滤低质内容。豆包搜索面向企业和开发者提供每月500次免费搜索额度，支持API、Skill、MCP等多种接入形态。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，今日，火山引擎宣布上线豆包搜索服务，提供跨语言、多模态、多垂类联网信息查询，融合全域互联网信息、行业专业知识以及字节跳动独家内容资源，为企业和开发者构建 AI Agent，提供更加智能、高效的信息引擎。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家附官方详细介绍如下： Agent 可信搜索：权威信息高效调用 围绕 Agent 在真实任务中的信息获取需求，豆包搜索针对搜索效率、信息可信度以及复杂任务处理能力进行了全面升级。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"// Agent 直接使用，提升信息获取效率 豆包搜索可 直接输出 Agent 能直接使用的信息格式 ，支持原文 markdown 和各类结构化数据返回；同时支持精准定向查询、宽搜语义改写、多轮检索增强等搜索模式。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"它能理解自然语言任务意图，跟随 Agent 任务推进自动调整检索策略，识别信息缺口并发起补充检索，无需开发者手动拆解查询指令。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"// 权威分级治理，提供可信信息来源 针对互联网低质内容和 SEO 灌水信息干扰检索结果的问题，豆包搜索从网站站点和创作者两个维度对信源建立权威分级体系，按行业分类做垂直治理；同时，融合全域互联网信息、行业知识与字节跳动独家内容资源， 从检索源头为 Agent 过滤无效低质信息，提升结果可信度 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]
- evidence_seed: {"company_actions":["火山引擎今日上线豆包搜索服务，为AI Agent提供跨语言、多模态联网信息查询，并融合字节跳动独家内容。该服务支持Agent直接使用的markdown与结构化数据输出，建立了信源权威分级体系以过滤低质内容。豆包搜索面向企业和开发者提供每月500次免费搜索额度，支持API、Skill、MCP等多种接入形态。","IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，今日，火山引擎宣布上线豆包搜索服务，提供跨语言、多模态、多垂类联网信息查询，融合全域互联网信息、行业专业知识以及字节跳动独家内容资源，为企业和开发者构建 AI Agent，提供更加智能、高效的信息引擎。","IT之家附官方详细介绍如下： Agent 可信搜索：权威信息高效调用 围绕 Agent 在真实任务中的信息获取需求，豆包搜索针对搜索效率、信息可信度以及复杂任务处理能力进行了全面升级。"],"case_details":[],"workflow_changes":[],"before_after_clues":["可能涉及 权限 / 安全治理 的前后变化，需要二搜补足变化前流程。"],"affected_roles":["开发者 / 工程团队"],"risks_or_constraints":[]}

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"Anthropic 旗下 Claude 的共享对话功能因未添加\"noindex\"标签，导致用户通过\"分享\"按钮生成的公开链接被谷歌等搜索引擎收录，暴露了个人简历、医疗记录、API 密钥等敏感信息。Anthropic 回应称共享链接本身不可猜测，仅当用户主动将链接发布到公开平台时才会被索引，并于 7 月 26 日为共享页面添加了\"noindex\"标签。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"supporting_context","text":"IT之家 7 月 29 日消息，7 月 26 日，Anthropic 旗 Claude 的共享对话功能曝出隐私风险。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"有人在 Reddit 上发帖称，Claude 用户通过“分享”功能生成的公开链接可被谷歌等搜索引擎进行收录，导致包含个人简历、健康状况、公司内部资料乃至 API 密钥等敏感信息的对话记录被公开。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"此次事件源于 Claude 的对话分享功能。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"用户点击聊天界面右上角的“分享”按钮后，系统会生成一个指向该对话快照的公开链接，任何拥有链接的人均可查看。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"问题在于，这些公开页面未添加“noindex”标签来禁止搜索引擎收录。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"quote","text":"Amazon is scaling back most of its in-house Nova AI models, including Nova Premier, Omni, Reel, and Canvas. - key_excerpts: [{"type":"opinion","text":"Meta CEO 扎克伯格抨击 Anthropic 和 OpenAI 以高度受控的方式开发 AI，认为这会导致权力集中、扼杀创新并缩小技术获取渠道。他主张让更多人而非更少人获得 AI 使用权，并呼吁为每个人提供\"个性化超级智能\"，而非追求单一\"友善\"的超级 AI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"北京时间 7 月 29 日，据《纽约时报》报道，Meta CEO 马克 · 扎克伯格 (Mark Zuckerberg) 公开发声，抨击世界上两家最具价值的 AI 创业公司 Anthropic 和 OpenAI，称它们开发 AI 软件的方式可能导致权力集中，从而缩小人们获取这一变革性技术的渠道。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"扎克伯格 现年 42 岁的扎克伯格在周二接受了采访，并未直接点名 Anthropic 和 OpenAI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"但他表示，如果领先的研究实验室想要以高度受控的方式开发 AI 技术，那就等同于在美国技术发展中“放弃我们的价值观”，并会扼杀创新。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"目前，Anthropic 和 OpenAI 正在以扎克伯格所说的方式开发 AI。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"“许多其他正在开发这项技术的实验室进行的讨论，很大程度上都充满了末日式的悲观情绪。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"funding","text":"Unity中国CEO张俊波表示，AI不会颠覆游戏引擎，反而能降低开发门槛。他认为世界模型和AI 3D生成模型更像是资产，游戏需要玩法、数值等系统工程，不相信\"一句话生成游戏\"能实现。团结引擎2.0已重构底层架构，并新增对PlayStation 5、XBOX Series X|S等主机平台的支持。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，据第一财经今天报道，Unity 中国 CEO 张俊波在团结引擎 2.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"product_update","text":"0 发布会后表示，他部分认同 AI 会颠覆软件、大规模提高生产效率， 但并不认为 AI 会颠覆游戏引擎 。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"quote","text":"他对此表示：“AI 对引擎公司来说反而是好事”。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"AI 能降低游戏开发和使用引擎的门槛，也意味着引擎公司可以在游戏行业里渗透得更深入。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"从长远看，世界模型确实能快速生成虚拟空间，但能够观看和体验的世界，并不等同于一款可以长期运营的游戏。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"case_detail","text":"智元自研的具身原生全模态大模型 WITA-Omni Preview 以 85.21 分综合成绩登顶 DailyOmni 榜单，在八项细分指标中的六项取得第一，超过千问、Gemini、豆包、英伟达等模型。该模型采用 Thinker-Talker-Actor 架构，将物理动作和表情提升为与语音并列的一级输出，并通过千万小时级多模态数据训练实现音视频联合理解与时序推理。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"IT之家 7 月 28 日消息，据智元 AGIBOT 微信公众号消息，近日，具身全模态理解权威榜单 DailyOmni 最新评测结果揭晓，智元自研的具身原生全模态大模型 WITA-Omni Preview，凭借领先的音视频联合理解与时序推理能力，以 85.","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"21 分综合成绩登顶榜首，超过千问、Gemini、豆包、英伟达等国内外领先模型，并在八项细分指标中的六项取得第一。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"据IT之家了解，DailyOmni 是行业公认的检验音视频时序对齐、跨模态联合推理能力的权威第三方榜单。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"case_detail","text":"不同于面向图文、短视频的常规评测任务，该榜单大量采用真实开放环境音视频素材，核心考验模型融合视觉画面、环境音频信息，精准识别声画对应关系、事件先后顺序及跨模态语义的综合能力，高度贴合人形机器人在真实物理空间开展人机交互所需的核心感知能力。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","case"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"官方称，WITA-Omni 领先的关键，在于它并不是简单地把聊天模型“装进”机器人，而是将物理动作和表情提升为与语音并列的一级输出，用一个原生端到端模型统一驱动感知、决策与表达，从 Thinker–Talker 范式上进一步扩展出面向具身输出的 Thinker–Talker–Actor 架构。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"workflow_change","text":"OpenAI CEO 萨姆·奥尔特曼认为，外界并不想要 AI CEO，因为公司决策需要明确的责任人。他指出，人们更珍视人与人之间的联结，即使 AI 已能胜任顾问、销售或工程师等角色，大多数人仍更愿意与真人打交道。奥尔特曼坦言，这一观察改变了他对 AI 将造成大规模失业的早期判断，并称自己\"很高兴判断错了\"。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"IT之家 7 月 29 日消息，据《商业内幕》今天（29 日）报道，OpenAI CEO 萨姆 · 奥尔特曼认为，人们并不希望让 AI 取代自己的职位。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"“以我的工作为例，外界希望知道谁要为一家公司的决策负责；如果决策失误，又该向谁追责。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"” 相较以往，奥尔特曼对这一问题的看法已经悄然发生了变化。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"据IT之家了解，去年 11 月，他曾在播客节目《与泰勒对话》中称，如果其他公司抢在 OpenAI 之前用 AI 模型取代 CEO，对 OpenAI 而言无异于一种失败。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"“如果 OpenAI 不是第一家 由 AI CEO 管理的大公司 ，我就该感到羞愧。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"company_action","text":"我们在 API 中引入了两种新的转录模型： • GPT-Live-Transcribe：专为低延迟实时转录而构建。 • GPT-Transcribe：针对已完成音频文件和批量工作负载的异步转录进行了优化。 两种模型都能更好地理解上下文，并在跨口音和语言的实际音频上提供更准确的转录，包括短句、数字、专业术语以及背景噪音较大的语音。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"我们在 API 中引入了两种新的转录模型： • GPT-Live-Transcribe：专为低延迟实时转录而构建。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"• GPT-Transcribe：针对已完成音频文件和批量工作负载的异步转录进行了优化。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"两种模型都能更好地理解上下文，并在跨口音和语言的实际音频上提供更准确的转录，包括短句、数字、专业术语以及背景噪音较大的语音。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"high","confidence":"medium"}]

- key_excerpts: [{"type":"opinion","text":"德里高等法院认定 OpenAI 利用亚洲国际新闻（ANI）社的内容训练人工智能不构成侵犯版权。法官 Amit Bansal 认为该行为符合印度《版权法》中研究类“合理使用”例外情形，且 ANI 未能证明 ChatGPT 直接复制其受版权保护内容。法院同时指出，现阶段颁布临时禁令将不利于印度正在开发的 LLM 及大量免费使用 ChatGPT 的用户。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"德里高等法院认定 OpenAI 利用亚洲国际新闻（ANI）社的内容训练人工智能不构成侵犯版权。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"opinion","text":"法官 Amit Bansal 认为该行为符合印度《版权法》中研究类“合理使用”例外情形，且 ANI 未能证明 ChatGPT 直接复制其受版权保护内容。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","viewpoint"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"},{"type":"company_action","text":"法院同时指出，现阶段颁布临时禁令将不利于印度正在开发的 LLM 及大量免费使用 ChatGPT 的用户。","supports":["signal_card_candidate","relationship_graph_input","business_change","case","trend_candidate_context"],"importance":"medium","confidence":"medium"}]
